## Portal Path Endpoint: Sub-System Node Channel
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is a part of a student portal, specifically the "CORE" section. It controls various transactional parameters related to student schedules, notifications, reward points, workflow requests, approvals, bookings, and services. The specific actions and parameters controlled by this page include:

- **Schedule Management**: Allows students to view and manage their schedules.
- **Circulars**: Displays notifications or circulars.
- **My Reward Points**: Shows the student's reward points.
- **Workflow Requests**: Manages workflow requests.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Manages workflow approvals.
- **Bookings**: Manages bookings.
- **Services**: Manages services.
- **Mentees**: Manages mentees.
- **Mentorship**: Manages mentorship.

#### 2. Data Element Block, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value
The page is structured with a left-side menu and a main content area. The left-side menu contains various tabs and sections, each representing different functionalities. The main content area displays the calendar for October 2026, showing the days of the week and the dates.

- **Left Side Menu**:
  - **Schedule**: Displays the schedule for the selected date.
  - **Circulars**: Displays notifications or circulars.
  - **My Reward Points**: Shows the student's reward points.
  - **Workflow Requests**: Manages workflow requests.
  - **Workflow Approvals**: Manages workflow approvals.
  - **Bookings**: Manages bookings.
  - **Services**: Manages services.
  - **Mentees**: Manages mentees.
  - **Mentorship**: Manages mentorship.

- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Calendar**: Displays the calendar for October 2026.
  - **Date Selection**: Allows the user to select a specific date.
  - **Notification**: Displays a message indicating "No schedule found for this date" for the selected date.

#### 3. Structural Functionality and Wider System Integration
This layout segment serves the wider system functions by providing a user-friendly interface for students to manage their academic and administrative tasks. The page is designed to be accessible and user-friendly, allowing students to easily navigate through different functionalities without needing to leave the main page.

- **User Interface**: The layout ensures that all functionalities are easily accessible from the main page, reducing the need for users to navigate through multiple pages.
- **Notifications and Alerts**: The page includes notifications and alerts, such as "No schedule found for this date," to inform users when specific functionalities are not available for the selected date.
- **Integration with Wider System**: The page integrates with the wider system by providing access to various functionalities, such as schedule management, workflow requests, and mentorship, which are essential for the student's academic and administrative needs.

Overall, this page is designed to streamline the student's experience by providing a comprehensive view of their academic and administrative tasks, ensuring that they can manage their schedules, notifications, and other important information from a single page.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Academics
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academics/people_schedule/#
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
The page is a student portal for managing academic schedules, workflow requests, and other academic-related tasks. The specific transactional parameters include:
- **Schedule Management**: Allows students to view and manage their academic schedules.
- **Workflow Requests**: Enables students to submit and track workflow requests.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Facilitates the approval process for workflow requests.
- **Bookings**: Manages student bookings for events or appointments.
- **Services**: Provides access to various academic services.
- **Mentees**: Manages mentees and mentorship programs.
- **Mentorship**: Manages mentorship relationships and activities.
- **Logged in as**: Displays the user's login information.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value Observations
The page is structured with a left-hand menu and a main content area. The main content area displays the current month's schedule for October 2026. The left-hand menu contains various tabs for different functionalities:
- **Schedule**: Displays the student's schedule for the current month.
- **Circulars**: Shows circulars or announcements.
- **My Reward Points**: Displays the student's reward points.
- **Workflow Requests**: Manages workflow requests.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Tracks workflow approvals.
- **Bookings**: Manages bookings.
- **Services**: Accesses various academic services.
- **Mentees**: Manages mentees.
- **Mentorship**: Manages mentorship relationships.

The main content area shows the current date as October 4, 2026, with the days of the week labeled (THU for Thursday, FRI for Friday, SAT for Saturday, SUN for Sunday, MON for Monday, TUE for Tuesday, WED for Wednesday). The page indicates that no schedule is found for the specified date.

#### 3. Structural Functionality and System Integration
The layout serves the wider system functions by:
- **Scheduling Management**: Facilitates students to view and manage their academic schedules, ensuring they are aware of upcoming classes, exams, and other academic commitments.
- **Workflow Requests**: Allows students to submit and track workflow requests, which could be related to academic processes such as course registration, exam scheduling, or other administrative tasks.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Enables students to monitor and approve workflow requests, ensuring that academic processes are efficiently managed and resolved.
- **Bookings**: Manages student bookings for events or appointments, ensuring that students can plan their time effectively.
- **Services**: Provides access to various academic services, such as counseling, counseling, and other support services.
- **Mentees and Mentorship**: Facilitates the management of mentees and mentorship relationships, promoting academic support and guidance.
- **Logged in as**: Displays the user's login information, ensuring that the user's identity is verified and their actions are traceable.

Overall, this layout is designed to streamline academic management, ensuring that students have easy access to all necessary information and tools to manage their academic schedules and processes efficiently.

### 1. Actions or Specific Transactional Parameters Controlled
This page controls the following specific transactional parameters:
- **Activity Log**: Displays the activity history of the user from September 28, 2026, to October 4, 2026.
- **No activity recorded in the last seven days**: Indicates that there has been no activity recorded in the last seven days.

### 2. Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value Observed
The page is structured as follows:
- **Header**: Contains the user's IP address and a user profile icon.
- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Title**: "Activity Log"
  - **Subtitle**: "Your activity from Sept. 28, 2026 to Oct. 4, 2026."
  - **Table**:
    - **Columns**: Date/Time, Activity, Details, IP Address
    - **Rows**: Currently, there are no rows in the table, indicating no activity recorded in the last seven days.
- **Footer**: Contains the user's login information and a copyright notice.

### 3. Structural Functionality and How It Serves the Wider System Functions
This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:
- **Displaying Activity History**: It provides a clear view of the user's activity over a specified period, helping the user understand their recent actions.
- **Indicating No Activity**: By showing "No activity recorded in the last seven days," it informs the user that there has been no significant activity in the specified period, which can be useful for monitoring and managing user behavior.
- **User Profile Information**: The footer with the user's login information and credentials helps in maintaining user authentication and security.

Overall, this page is designed to provide a clear and concise view of the user's activity history, ensuring that users can easily monitor and manage their activities within the system.

- **Refresh Button**: Allows users to refresh the page to update the session list.

  - **Title**: "Active Sessions"
  - **Description**: "Review browsers and devices currently signed in to your account."
  - **Refresh Button**: Positioned on the right side of the title.
  - **User Information**: Displays the user's name and possibly other details.

- **Main Content Area**:

  - **Details**: "nginx/1.18.0 (Ubuntu)"

    - **Schedule**: Displays the number of scheduled sessions (43).
    - **Circulars**: Displays the number of circulars (43).
    - **My Reward Points**: Displays the user's reward points.
    - **Workflow Requests**: Displays the number of workflow requests.
    - **Workflow Approvals**: Displays the number of workflow approvals.

    - **Services**: Displays the number of services.

- **Footer Section**:
  - **Copyright Information**: Displays the copyright details of the website.

- **Session Management**: The page serves as a central hub for managing active sessions. It allows users to review and manage their current sessions, which are essential for tracking and monitoring user activity.
- **User Interface**: The layout is designed to be user-friendly, with clear sections for different types of sessions and a sidebar for quick access to other functionalities.
- **Error Handling**: The page handles temporary unavailability by displaying a clear error message, which helps users understand the issue and provides a way to refresh the page for updates.
- **Security**: The page ensures that only logged-in users can access the active sessions, maintaining the security of the system.

Overall, this page is designed to provide a comprehensive view of active sessions, manage user sessions, and ensure that users can easily access and update their session information.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Services
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academics/services/
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to provide access to various operational tools for students, including:
- **Calculate My Attendance**: To calculate current academic slot attendance with session-level counting details.
- **LeaveBalance**: To view leave credits, debits, and current balance.
- **Skill Development**: To review accessible skills, LMS course links, marks, and earned reward points.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured into several key sections:
1. **Header Section**:
   - **Logo**: Located at the top left corner.
   - **User Information**: Located at the top right corner, showing the user's name and ID.
   - **User Profile**: Located at the top right corner, showing the user's profile picture and name.

2. **Main Content Section**:
   - **Title**: "Services" at the top.
   - **Description**: "Access user-facing operational tools from one place."
   - **Service Cards**:
     - **Calculate My Attendance**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.
     - **LeaveBalance**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.
     - **Skill Development**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.

3. **Footer Section**:
   - **Logged in as**: Displays the user's name and ID.
   - **Copyright**: Displays the copyright information for Saveetha Engineering College.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
This layout serves the wider system functions by:
- **Providing Access to Operational Tools**: Students can easily access tools for managing their attendance, leave balances, and skill development.
- **User Identification**: Ensures that the user is logged in and provides their profile information.
- **User Interface**: Offers a clean and organized interface for users to interact with the system.
- **System Integration**: Facilitates the integration of different operational tools into a single user interface, enhancing user experience.

### Detailed Breakdown

#### Header Section
- **Logo**: Positioned at the top left corner, representing the institution's logo.
- **User Information**: Positioned at the top right corner, showing the user's name and ID.
- **User Profile**: Positioned at the top right corner, showing the user's profile picture and name.

#### Main Content Section

- **Service Cards**:
  - **Calculate My Attendance**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.
  - **LeaveBalance**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.
  - **Skill Development**: Card with an icon, title, and a "Open service" button.

#### Footer Section

- **Copyright**: Displays the copyright information for Saveetha Engineering College.

### Conclusion
This page is designed to provide students with easy access to various operational tools for managing their attendance, leave balances, and skill development. The structured layout ensures that users can easily navigate and interact with the system, while the footer provides essential information about the user's login status and the institution's copyright.

## Portal Path Endpoint: EventsParticipating
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academicevents/event-participating/
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to manage and display events that a user is participating in. The user can filter events based on specific dates, view detailed session information, and manage their participation status. The page also allows users to reset their filters and apply the selected filters.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value Observations
- **Header Section:**
  - **Title:** "Events Participating"
  - **Date Range:** From 09/19/2026 to 10/19/2026
  - **Buttons:** "Reset" and "Apply"

- **Filter Section:**
  - **Date Range:** From Date (09/19/2026) and To Date (10/19/2026)
  - **Dropdown:** "My Reward Points"

- **Event List:**
  - **Events:** 19AI305 - Advanced C Programming, 19CS405 - Operating System, 19CS406 - Computer Networks
  - **Details:**
    - **Event Term:** 26-27-ODD-EXAMHALL
    - **Event Type:** EXAMINATIONS
    - **Date:** September 29, 2026, September 30, 2026, October 3, 2026
    - **Time:** 9 a.m. - noon
    - **Location:** 4372, 4483, 5451
    - **Mode:** Booked
    - **Unread Chat:** 0

- **Buttons:**
  - **"View Session Details"** for each event

- **Footer Section:**
  - **Logged in as:** Placeholder text

#### 3. Structural Functionality and System Integration
- **Filtering and Sorting:**
  - The user can filter events based on specific dates, which helps in organizing and managing their schedule efficiently.
  - The "Reset" and "Apply" buttons allow users to clear or confirm their date range selections.

- **Event Details:**
  - Each event has detailed information such as the event term, type, date, time, location, and mode of participation.
  - The "View Session Details" button provides access to more detailed information about each event, which is crucial for students to understand their participation status and schedule.

- **User Interface:**
  - The layout is designed to be user-friendly, with clear sections for filtering, viewing, and managing events.
  - The use of buttons and dropdowns ensures that the user interface is intuitive and easy to navigate.

- **System Integration:**
  - This page is part of a larger system that manages student events and schedules.
  - The system likely integrates with other parts of the student portal, such as the calendar, messaging system, and reward point tracking.
  - The system uses the date range filter to dynamically update the list of events based on the selected dates, ensuring that the user always sees the most relevant events.

### Summary
This page is a critical component of a student portal, designed to help students manage their participation in academic events. It allows users to filter events based on specific dates, view detailed session information, and manage their participation status. The page is structured to be user-friendly and integrates seamlessly with other parts of the system, ensuring a smooth and efficient user experience.

- **Bookings**: Allows the user to view and manage bookings.

- **Mentees**: Allows the user to manage their mentees.
- **Mentorship**: Provides access to mentorship-related activities.

The page is structured as follows:
- **Header**: Contains the user's profile and session information.
- **Left Sidebar**: Contains navigation links for different sections of the portal.
- **Main Content Area**: Displays the activity log with a table structure.
  - **Table Headers**: "Date/Time," "Activity," "Details," and "IP Address."
  - **Table Body**: Displays the activity log with a message indicating no activity recorded in the last seven days.

### 3. Structural Functionality and Wider System Integration

- **Displaying Activity Logs**: Helps users track their past activities and understand what they have done.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Academics
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/rewards/my-reward-points/#
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
This screenshot appears to be from a student portal, specifically the "My Reward Points" section. Here is a detailed breakdown of the page layout and its functions:

### Actions or Specific Transactional Parameters
1. **Reward Points Tracking**: The page allows users to track their reward points, credits, and balances over different reward terms.
2. **Reward Terms**: Users can apply filters to view rewards for specific terms or all terms.
3. **Date Range**: Users can select a date range to view rewards within that period.
4. **Description**: Each reward entry includes a description of the reward, such as the module or module level being claimed.
5. **Balance Before**: The balance before the selected date is displayed, showing the total reward points before any deductions or credits.
6. **Closing Balance**: The closing balance is shown at the end, indicating the total reward points as of the selected date.

### Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value
1. **Header Section**:
   - **Title**: "My Reward Points"
   - **Description**: "Track your credits, debits, and balance by reward term."
   - **Filters**: "Reward Term" with options to select all terms or specific terms.
   - **Date Range**: "Date" with a date picker to select a date range.
   - **Apply Button**: "Apply" button to apply filters and view rewards.

2. **Table Structure**:
   - **Columns**: 
     - **# Reward Term**: The sequence number of the reward term.
     - **Date**: The date the reward was claimed.
     - **Opening Balance**: The balance before the selected date.
     - **Debit**: The amount credited to the reward.
     - **Credit**: The amount debited from the reward.
     - **Balance**: The current balance after the debit and credit.
     - **Description**: A brief description of the reward claim.

3. **Score Layout**:
   - **List of Rewards**: Each row in the table represents a reward claim, showing the date, balance before, debit, credit, and balance after the claim.
   - **Total Balance**: The closing balance is shown at the bottom, indicating the total reward points as of the selected date.

### Structural Functionality
1. **User Interface**: The page is designed to be user-friendly, allowing students to easily track their reward points and understand how they have accumulated over time.
2. **Data Management**: The table structure helps in managing and tracking reward points efficiently, ensuring that students can see their progress and any changes in their balance.
3. **Reporting**: The closing balance at the end provides a comprehensive overview of the student's reward points, which can be used for reporting or auditing purposes.

### Conclusion
This page serves as a critical tool for students to manage and track their reward points, ensuring transparency and accountability in their academic and extracurricular activities. The structured layout and clear descriptions help in understanding the impact of each reward claim on the overall balance.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Academics
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academicevents/event-managing/#
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
The page is designed to manage and view event sessions assigned to the user. The specific transactional parameters include:
- **Event Term**: Selects the specific event term or category.
- **From Date**: Sets the start date for the event sessions.
- **To Date**: Sets the end date for the event sessions.
- **Apply Button**: Submits the selected filters to display the relevant event sessions.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value Observations
The page contains the following data elements and layout components:
- **Event Term Dropdown**: Displays a list of event terms.
- **From Date Input**: Allows the user to select the start date.
- **To Date Input**: Allows the user to select the end date.
- **Apply Button**: Submits the selected filters.
- **Reset Button**: Resets the filters to default values.
- **Message Box**: Displays a message indicating "No event sessions found for the selected filters."

#### 3. Structural Functionality and System Integration
The layout serves the following broader system functions:
- **Event Management**: Allows users to manage and view event sessions assigned to them.
- **Date Filtering**: Enables users to filter event sessions based on specific dates.
- **User Interface**: Provides a user-friendly interface for managing event sessions.
- **System Integration**: Connects to the broader system for displaying relevant event sessions based on the selected filters.

### Detailed Breakdown

#### Event Term Dropdown
The dropdown menu allows users to select the specific event term or category they are interested in. This dropdown is crucial for narrowing down the event sessions to relevant ones.

#### From Date Input
The date input field allows users to specify the start date for the event sessions. This is essential for filtering events that occurred within a specific time frame.

#### To Date Input
The date input field also allows users to specify the end date for the event sessions. This is used to filter events that occurred within a specific time frame.

#### Apply Button
The "Apply" button is used to submit the selected filters. When clicked, it triggers the display of event sessions that match the selected filters.

#### Reset Button
The "Reset" button allows users to clear the selected filters and return to the default state. This is useful for troubleshooting or starting over.

#### Message Box
The message box displays a message indicating that no event sessions were found for the selected filters. This helps users understand the current state of their filters and whether they need to adjust them.

### System Integration
The page is integrated with the broader system to display relevant event sessions based on the selected filters. This ensures that users can efficiently manage and view their assigned event sessions.

### Conclusion
The page is designed to manage and view event sessions assigned to the user, with specific transactional parameters for filtering events based on dates. The layout is user-friendly and integrates with the broader system to provide accurate and relevant information.

### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Transactional Parameters
The page is designed to manage and communicate within the context of the IndiaSkills Competition 2026-27. It controls specific actions related to registration, mentorship, and communication within the competition. Key transactional parameters include:
- **Registration and Mentorship**: The page manages the registration of participants and mentors, as well as the assignment of mentors to participants.
- **Communication**: It facilitates group chats, direct messages, and task tracking.
- **Task Completion**: It tracks the completion of tasks and provides updates on the progress of the competition.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured with several key data elements and tables:
- **Participants and Mentors**: Displays the total number of participants and mentors.
- **Task Completion**: Shows the number of tasks completed and the expected number.
- **Circulars**: Lists the total number of circulars and the specific chat and task details.
- **Group Chat**: Displays the current chat activity and the number of messages.
- **Discussion Topics**: Lists the focused threaded discussions.
- **Direct Messages**: Shows the direct messages exchanged.
- **Schedule**: Displays the schedule for the competition.
- **Reward Points**: Shows the reward points available.
- **Workflow Requests**: Manages workflow requests.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Tracks workflow approvals.
- **Bookings**: Manages bookings.
- **Services**: Provides access to various services.
- **Mentees**: Manages mentees.
- **Mentorship**: Manages mentorship.

#### 3. Score Layout and Variable Values
The page displays the following score layout and variable values:
- **Participants**: 3491 / 4463
- **Mentors**: 108 / 111
- **Managers**: 4 / 4
- **Start Date**: 03/09/2026
- **Task Completion**: 0/8926
- **Circulars**: 126
- **Tasks**: 2
- **Rating**: 0.0 / 5 (0)

#### 4. Structured Functionality
The page serves the wider system functions by:
- **Registration and Mentorship Management**: Facilitates the registration of participants and mentors, ensuring that the correct mentors are assigned to participants.
- **Communication**: Provides a platform for participants to communicate with mentors, discuss topics, and manage direct messages.
- **Task Tracking**: Tracks the completion of tasks and updates participants on their progress.
- **Schedule and Reward Points**: Displays the schedule and reward points available, helping participants manage their time and earn points.
- **Workflow Management**: Manages workflow requests and approvals, ensuring that all processes are followed and tracked.
- **Booking and Services**: Manages bookings and provides access to various services, ensuring that all resources are available to participants.
- **Mentee and Mentor Management**: Manages mentees and mentors, ensuring that the correct mentees are assigned to mentors.

### Summary
The page is a comprehensive management tool for the IndiaSkills Competition 2026-27, designed to facilitate registration, communication, task tracking, and workflow management. It serves as a central hub for participants to access all necessary information and tools to participate in the competition effectively.

### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to manage and display academic circulars for a student. The specific actions and transactional parameters controlled by this page include:
- **Circulars Management**: Users can view, edit, and manage circulars related to their academic progress.
- **Role Selection**: Users can filter circulars based on their role (e.g., AcademicMentor, NoMentor).

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to manage and display student attendance and feedback for a specific event, "19CS406 - Computer Networks." The page controls the following specific actions and parameters:
- **Event Details**: Displays the event name, date, time, and booking status.
- **Attendance Status**: Shows the attendance percentage and status.
- **Feedback Status**: Indicates whether the feedback is pending or has been submitted.
- **Booking Information**: Provides the booking date and time.
- **Student Details**: Lists the student's name, registration number, and mode of attendance.
- **Summary**: Displays the attendance percentage and feedback status in a summary format.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured into several data element blocks and tables:
- **Header Block**: Contains the event name, date, and time.
- **Student Information Block**: Displays the student's name, registration number, and mode of attendance.
- **Event Details Block**: Provides the event details, including the event term, location, and time.
- **Attendance Summary Block**: Shows the attendance percentage and feedback status in a summary format.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:
- **Displaying Event Details**: Ensures that all relevant information about the event is visible to the student and the institution.
- **Monitoring Attendance**: Allows the institution to monitor and manage student attendance.
- **Feedback Tracking**: Facilitates the tracking and submission of student feedback.
- **Student Information Management**: Provides a centralized place for students to view their attendance and feedback status.
- **System Integration**: Ensures that the system can be accessed and managed by students and administrators, facilitating the broader system functions.

### Detailed Breakdown

- **Event Name**: "19CS406 - Computer Networks"
- **Date and Time**: "26-27-ODD-EXAMALL - EXAMINATIONS" (Oct 3, 2026, 9 a.m. - noon)

This page is a critical component of the student portal, ensuring that students can easily access and manage their attendance and feedback for specific events. #### Main Content

  - **Days**: From Tuesday (3) to Sunday (9).

  - **No Schedule Found**: Indicates that no schedule is found for the highlighted day.

  - **Schedule**: Displays the schedule for the selected day.
  - **Circulars**: Displays the number of circulars.
  - **My Reward Points**: Displays the user's reward points.

- **Highlighted Day**: The highlighted day (Friday, November 6) indicates the current day of interest, which is crucial for displaying relevant information and facilitating user actions.
- **Sidebar Navigation**: The sidebar provides quick access to various sections of the system, such as schedules, notifications, and other important information. This helps users navigate efficiently and access the necessary functionalities.

#### Wider System Functions
- **User Management**: The page allows users to manage their schedules, view their reward points, and access other important information. This is essential for maintaining a user-friendly interface.
- **Event Management**: The calendar and schedule sections help manage and view events, appointments, and other scheduled activities, ensuring that users can plan and organize their time effectively.
- **Notification System**: The circulars section helps users stay informed about important updates and announcements, which is crucial for maintaining communication within the system.
- **Workflow Management**: The workflow-related sections help manage and track the progress of workflows, ensuring that tasks are completed on time and efficiently.

Overall, this layout segment serves the wider system functions by providing a comprehensive and user-friendly interface for managing schedules, events, and other important information, while also facilitating easy navigation and access to various functionalities.

### Structured Functionality

1. **Attendance Management**:
   - The page manages the attendance of students in a course. It includes entries for both IN and OUT of the campus (OTP), which are essential for tracking student attendance.

2. **Lesson Delivery**:
   - The page details the lessons taught, including specific topics covered in each session. This helps in tracking the progress of students in their course.

3. **Class Feedback**:
   - The page collects and records class feedback, which is crucial for improving teaching methods and student engagement.

4. **Scheduled Class Details**:
   - The page provides detailed information about the scheduled class, including the start and end times, and the date.

5. **OTP Entry**:

6. **Approval Process**:
   - The page handles the approval process for both IN and OUT OTPs, ensuring that only approved students are allowed to enter the campus.

7. **Feedback Submission**:
   - The page allows students to submit feedback on the class, which is essential for collecting student opinions and suggestions.

### Conclusion
This page serves as a comprehensive tool for managing student attendance, lesson delivery, and class feedback in an educational institution. It ensures that all necessary steps are taken to maintain a secure and effective learning environment.

### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters

This page is a student portal page that controls various academic and administrative activities for students. The specific transactional parameters include:

- **Schedule**: Displays the student's academic schedule for the selected day.
- **Circulars**: Shows the latest circulars or announcements.
- **My Reward Points**: Displays the student's reward points.
- **Workflow Requests**: Manages workflow requests.
- **Workflow Approvals**: Manages workflow approvals.
- **Bookings**: Manages bookings.
- **Services**: Provides access to various services.
- **Manage**: Manages mentees and mentorship.
- **Logged in as**: Displays the user's logged-in information.

### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure

The page is structured as follows:

1. **Header**:
   - Contains the page title and navigation bar.
   - Displays the current date and time.

2. **Main Content Area**:
   - **Calendar**: Displays the calendar for the selected month (August 2026).
   - **Schedule**: Displays the student's schedule for the selected day (Wednesday, August 26, 2026).
     - **Course Name**: "19CS405 - Operating System"
     - **Slot**: "26OD1089"
     - **Time**: "08:00 AM - 09:59 AM"
     - **Venue**: "3483"
     - **Leave Status**: "Leave Rejected"
     - **Leave Reason**: "OD Approval For Completion of Modules (Leave)"
   - **Circulars**: Displays the latest circulars.
   - **My Reward Points**: Displays the student's reward points.
   - **Workflow Requests**: Displays the workflow requests.
   - **Workflow Approvals**: Displays the workflow approvals.
   - **Bookings**: Displays the bookings.
   - **Services**: Displays the available services.
   - **Manage**: Displays the mentees and mentorship.

3. **Footer**:
   - Contains the copyright information and other legal notices.

### 3. Structural Functionality

This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:

1. **Displaying Academic Information**: It provides a clear view of the student's academic schedule, leave status, and other relevant information.
2. **Managing Workflow**: It allows students to manage their workflow requests and approvals, ensuring transparency and accountability.
3. **Booking System**: It facilitates the booking of services and appointments, ensuring that students can plan their activities efficiently.
4. **Mentorship and Mentees**: It provides a platform for students to manage their mentorship relationships, fostering a supportive learning environment.
5. **User Management**: It allows administrators to manage user information, ensuring that the system is secure and up-to-date.

Overall, this layout segment is designed to provide students with a comprehensive view of their academic and administrative activities, while also ensuring that the system is user-friendly and efficient.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Sub-System Node Channel
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academicevents/event_attendance_process/?slotsession_id=15170&year=2026&month=9&day=3
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to manage event attendance for a specific session. The key actions and parameters include:
- **Event Attendance Tracking**: Users can track their attendance status.
- **OTP Entry**: Users need to enter an OTP to confirm their attendance.
- **Approval Status**: The system waits for approval from the organizer.
- **Feedback Submission**: Users can submit feedback about the event.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured with a series of data elements and tables that provide a clear timeline of events and statuses. The main sections are:
- **Event Started**: Scheduled from 10:00 AM to 10:59 AM on Thu, 03 Sep 2026.
- **IN OTP Entry**: The user needs to enter the OTP shared by the organizer.
- **IN APPROVAL**: Waiting for organizer approval.
- **OUT OTP Entry**: The user needs to enter the OUT OTP (if enabled).
- **OUT APPROVAL**: Waiting for OUT approval.
- **Feedback Submission**: Submit event feedback.
- **ATTENDANCE RECORDED**: The attendance is recorded once the feedback is submitted.

#### 3. Variable Value and Structure
The page uses a timeline format to display the sequence of events:
- **Event Started**: Scheduled from 10:00 AM to 10:59 AM.
- **IN OTP Entry**: Scheduled on Thu, 03 Sep 2026.
- **IN APPROVAL**: Waiting for organizer approval.
- **OUT OTP Entry**: Scheduled on Thu, 03 Sep 2026.
- **OUT APPROVAL**: Waiting for OUT approval.
- **Feedback Submission**: Submit event feedback.
- **ATTENDANCE RECORDED**: The attendance is recorded once the feedback is submitted.

#### 4. Structural Functionality
The page serves the wider system functions by:
- **Event Tracking**: Users can track their attendance status and confirm it with an OTP.
- **Approval Process**: The system waits for approval from the organizer, ensuring the event is properly managed.
- **Feedback Collection**: Users can provide feedback, which is essential for improving future events.
- **Attendance Recording**: The system records attendance once the feedback is submitted, ensuring accurate tracking.

### Detailed Breakdown

#### Event Started
- **Scheduled Time**: 10:00 AM to 10:59 AM
- **Date**: Thu, 03 Sep 2026

#### IN OTP Entry
- **Scheduled Time**: Thu, 03 Sep 2026
- **Action**: Enter the OTP shared by the organizer

#### IN APPROVAL
- **Status**: Waiting for organizer approval

#### OUT OTP Entry
- **Scheduled Time**: Thu, 03 Sep 2026
- **Action**: Enter the OUT OTP (if enabled)

#### OUT APPROVAL
- **Status**: Waiting for OUT approval

#### Feedback Submission
- **Action**: Submit event feedback

#### ATTENDANCE RECORDED
- **Status**: Attendance is recorded once the feedback is submitted

### Conclusion
This page is designed to manage event attendance efficiently, ensuring that users can track their attendance, confirm it with an OTP, and provide feedback. The structured timeline and clear status updates help in maintaining a smooth approval process and accurate attendance records.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Sub-System Node Channel
- **Direct Link Target:** https://lms2.ai.saveetha.in/login/forgot_password.php
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters

This page is designed to facilitate the user's password reset process. Users can either search for their account using their username or email address. The specific transactional parameters controlled by this page include:

- **Username Search**: Users can enter their username to find their account.
- **Email Search**: Users can enter their email address to find their account.
- **Password Reset Instructions**: If the user's account is found, an email will be sent to the specified email address with instructions on how to reset their password.

### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure

The page is divided into two main sections:

#### Search by Username
- **Username Input Field**: A text input field where users can enter their username.
- **Search Button**: A blue button labeled "Search" to initiate the search.

#### Search by Email Address
- **Email Input Field**: A text input field where users can enter their email address.
- **Search Button**: A blue button labeled "Search" to initiate the search.

### 3. Variable Value and Layout Structure

The page layout is designed to be user-friendly and responsive. Here is a breakdown of the layout structure:

- **Header**: The top section contains a brief description of the page's purpose.
- **Main Content Area**: The central area contains the search fields and buttons.
- **Footer**: The bottom section is empty and does not contain any additional content.

### 4. Structural Functionality

This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:

- **User Authentication**: It allows users to authenticate their account by either username or email.
- **Password Reset**: It provides a mechanism for users to reset their password if they have forgotten it.
- **User Experience**: The design ensures a smooth and intuitive user experience, guiding users through the password reset process efficiently.

### 5. Security Considerations

- **Input Validation**: The page does not show any form of input validation errors, which could be a security risk.
- **Error Handling**: There is no visible error handling mechanism, which could lead to potential security vulnerabilities if users are not informed of errors.
- **Security Measures**: The page does not implement any additional security measures such as CAPTCHA or two-factor authentication.

### 6. Accessibility

- **Accessibility Features**: The page is designed to be accessible, with clear labels for input fields and buttons.
- **Responsiveness**: The layout is responsive, ensuring it works well on various devices and screen sizes.

### 7. Potential Improvements

- **Error Handling**: Implementing error handling mechanisms to inform users of any issues with their search query.
- **Security Measures**: Adding security measures such as CAPTCHA to prevent automated attacks.
- **User Experience**: Enhancing the user experience by providing more detailed instructions or feedback if the search fails.

By addressing these areas, the page can become more robust, secure, and user-friendly.

7. **Contact Information**: Links to the institution's contact details, such as email, phone number, or physical address.

### Page Functions and Data Elements

1. **Navigation and Links:**
   - The navigation bar allows users to access different sections of the portal, such as settings, profile, activity log, college hours, active sessions, and logout.

2. **Core Sections:**
   - The "Core" section provides quick links to important sections of the portal, such as schedule, circulars, reward points, workflow requests, and approvals.
   - The "Manage" section allows users to access and manage their academic and administrative tasks, such as subjects, handling subjects, learning activities, events, and biometric logs.

3. **Participants and Managers:**

   - The "Managers" section shows the number of managers and their status.

   - The "Start Date" section shows the date when the task was started.
   - The "Task Completion" section shows the number of tasks completed and expected.

5. **Approved / Expected:**
   - The "Approved / Expected" section shows the number of tasks approved and expected.

### Page Purpose and Functionality

The page serves as a comprehensive student portal for managing and tracking academic activities and tasks. It provides quick access to different sections of the portal and allows users to manage their academic and administrative tasks. The page also displays important information such as the number of participants, mentors, managers, and the start date of tasks. - **Circulars and Reward Points:** Offers a way to manage and view important announcements and rewards.
- **Workflow Requests and Approvals:** Facilitates the management of workflow processes, ensuring that tasks are tracked and approved.
- **Bookings and Services:** Enables users to manage their bookings and access available services.
- **Mentees and Mentorship:** Facilitates the connection between students and mentors, providing support and guidance.
- **User Profile and Logout:** Provides user management options, including logging out to maintain security and privacy.

Overall, this page serves as a comprehensive interface for managing academic, administrative, and personal activities, ensuring that all relevant information is easily accessible and manageable for the user.

### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Page Layout and Functionality

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
The page is designed to manage and display details of a student's participation in an academic event. The specific transactional parameters include:
- **Event Participation**: The student is participating in the "Module/LeetCode/SA" event.
- **Event Date**: The event is scheduled for August 31, 2026, from 3 p.m. to 3:59 p.m.
- **Booking Status**: The booking is confirmed and booked.
- **Feedback Status**: The feedback has been posted.
- **Attendance Status**: The student is present with a 100% attendance rate.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured into several key sections:
- **Header**: Displays the event title, date, and booking information.
- **Overview**: Contains details about the student's participation, including their name, registration number, and booking mode.
- **Session Details**: Provides information about the event, including the event term, location, and time.
- **Summary**: Shows the attendance status and feedback status.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
The layout serves the wider system functions by:
- **Displaying Event Details**: Ensures that all relevant information about the event is visible, allowing students to understand the context and schedule.
- **Monitoring Student Attendance**: Facilitates tracking of student attendance, which is crucial for academic evaluations and performance tracking.
- **Feedback Management**: Allows for easy posting and management of student feedback, which can be used for continuous improvement and evaluation.
- **Booking Confirmation**: Ensures that students are aware of their booking status, which is important for logistical planning and resource allocation.

### Detailed Breakdown

#### Header
- **Event Title**: "Module/LeetCode/SA"
- **Event Date**: "26-27-ODD-EXAMCENTRE - EXAMATIONS"
- **Booking Date**: "Aug 31, 2026"
- **Booking Time**: "3 p.m. - 3:59 p.m."
- **Feedback Status**: "Feedback Posted"
- **Booking Status**: "Booked"
- **Back to Events**: A button to navigate back to the main event page.

#### Overview
- **Student Information**:
  - **Name**: MUHAMMAD ASJAD E
  - **Registration Number**: 212225240091
  - **Booking Mode**: Booked

#### Session Details
- **Event Title**: "Module/LeetCode/SA"
- **Event Term**: "26-27-ODD-EXAMCENTRE"
- **Location**: "1731"
- **Time**: "3 p.m. - 3:59 p.m."

#### Summary
- **Attendance Status**: "Present"
  - **Attendance Percentage**: "100%"
- **Feedback Status**: "Feedback Posted"

### Conclusion
This page is designed to provide a comprehensive overview of a student's participation in an academic event, ensuring that all relevant details are accessible and easily understandable. The structured layout facilitates easy navigation and access to critical information, which is essential for both students and administrators managing academic events.
